- **solicitation-analysis**: Analyze RFP/RFQ/IFB solicitation documents to extract requirements, evaluation criteria, compliance needs, and risks. - **solicitation-create-eval**: Internal /ace:run step — Grade the published solicitation against its PDD. ([dimagi-internal/ace](https://github.com/dimagi-internal/ace/tree/HEAD/skills/solicitation-create-eval) · ★1)
- **solicitation-doc-modifications-udm**: Given a solicitation text (markdown, typically OCR-derived) and a sponsor-defaults JSON object (the output of sponsor-doc-defaults-udm), emits the subset of document requirements the solicitation modifies and the net-new documents it introduces — as a structured JSON list. Use when a pre-award team has a baseline sponsor checklist and needs to adapt it to a specific program, when an ingest pipeline needs to resolve a solicitation's document requirements on top of a sponsor prior, or when a user asks "what does this solicitation change or add beyond the sponsor defaults?". Every entry links ba… ([AI4RA/prompt-library](https://github.com/AI4RA/prompt-library/tree/HEAD/components/solicitation-doc-modifications-udm/skill))
- **solicitation-edit**: Edit an existing labs solicitation: extend its deadline, rewrite its text or criteria, change its visibility, or close or reopen it. - **solicitation-google-form**: Mirror a labs solicitation into a public Google Form and import its responses. Needs a solicitation id. ([dimagi-internal/ace](https://github.com/dimagi-internal/ace/tree/HEAD/skills/solicitation-google-form) · ★1)
- **solicitation-google-form-qa**: Pass/fail check of a solicitation's public Google Form against the live call. ([dimagi-internal/ace](https://github.com/dimagi-internal/ace/tree/HEAD/skills/solicitation-google-form-qa) · ★1)
- **solicitation-monitor**: Check a solicitation's responses: count only (--quick), full pull (--monitor), or final pull (--close). Needs a solicitation id. ([dimagi-internal/ace](https://github.com/dimagi-internal/ace/tree/HEAD/skills/solicitation-monitor) · ★1)
- **solicitation-review**: Score a solicitation's responses and recommend an awardee; after human approval, record the award. Needs a solicitation with responses. ([dimagi-internal/ace](https://github.com/dimagi-internal/ace/tree/HEAD/skills/solicitation-review) · ★1)
- **solicitation-review-eval**: Internal /ace:run step — Compare ACE's top solicitation pick with the award. ([dimagi-internal/ace](https://github.com/dimagi-internal/ace/tree/HEAD/skills/solicitation-review-eval) · ★1)
- **solicitation-review-qa**: Structural QA on the solicitation-review recommendation + scoring artifacts before the HITL award_response gate. Binary pass/fail. Catches missing recommendation, unnamed awardee, incomplete scoring, unresolved tie-breaks. Static-only; no LLM. Gates the HITL human reviewer — runs BEFORE the irreversible award_response call. - **solicitation-seed-responses-qa**: Pass/fail check of seeded synthetic responses against live labs reads. Static, no LLM. ([dimagi-internal/ace](https://github.com/dimagi-internal/ace/tree/HEAD/skills/solicitation-seed-responses-qa) · ★1)
